<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 */
interface Animal
{
    /**
     * Declared here because a property TYPED as this union is serialized through it: the emitted
     * `toArray()` of an owning DTO calls `$this->prop?->toArray()`, which without this line is a call
     * to a method the interface does not have. Every member is a generated DTO and has it.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array;
}

===== ApiTest2PatchRequest.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use function floor;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Route: PATCH /api/test2
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ApiTest2PatchRequest::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ApiTest2PatchRequest::fromValidated($request->validated());
 */
final class ApiTest2PatchRequest
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly int $idPatchTest2,
        private readonly string $namePatchTest2,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'idPatchTest2' => ['present', 'integer'],
            'namePatchTest2' => ['present', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            idPatchTest2: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['idPatchTest2']),
            namePatchTest2: $data['namePatchTest2'],
        );

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getIdPatchTest2(): int
    {
        return $this->idPatchTest2;
    }

    public function getNamePatchTest2(): string
    {
        return $this->namePatchTest2;
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['idPatchTest2'] = $this->idPatchTest2;
        $result['namePatchTest2'] = $this->namePatchTest2;

        return $result;
    }
}

===== ApiTestCatsActionsGetQueryParams.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use function array_keys;
use function floor;
use function in_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Route: GET /api/test/cats/{id}/actions/{actionId}
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ApiTestCatsActionsGetQueryParams::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ApiTestCatsActionsGetQueryParams::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ApiTestCatsActionsGetQueryParams
{
    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param string $id Example: 00000000-1111-4222-8333-444455556666
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $id,
        private readonly int $actionId,
        private readonly ?int $page = null,
        private readonly ?int $limit = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => ['present', 'string', 'uuid'],
            'actionId' => ['present', 'integer'],
            'page' => ['sometimes', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'limit' => ['sometimes', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:100'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            id: $data['id'],
            actionId: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['actionId']),
            page: ($data['page'] ?? null) === null ? null : self::toIntIfIntegral($data['page']),
            limit: ($data['limit'] ?? null) === null ? null : self::toIntIfIntegral($data['limit']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getId(): string
    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    public function getActionId(): int
    {
        return $this->actionId;
    }

    public function getPage(): ?int
    {
        return $this->page;
    }

    public function isPageProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('page', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getLimit(): ?int
    {
        return $this->limit;
    }

    public function isLimitProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('limit', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['id'] = $this->id;
        $result['actionId'] = $this->actionId;

        if ($this->isPageProvided()) {
            $result['page'] = $this->page;
        }

        if ($this->isLimitProvided()) {
            $result['limit'] = $this->limit;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== ApiTestLegacyPostQueryParams.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use function array_keys;
use function in_array;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Route: POST /api/test/legacy
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ApiTestLegacyPostQueryParams::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ApiTestLegacyPostQueryParams::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ApiTestLegacyPostQueryParams
{
    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly ?bool $legacyFlag = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'legacyFlag' => ['sometimes', 'boolean'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            legacyFlag: ($data['legacyFlag'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['legacyFlag'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getLegacyFlag(): ?bool
    {
        return $this->legacyFlag;
    }

    public function isLegacyFlagProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('legacyFlag', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];

        if ($this->isLegacyFlagProvided()) {
            $result['legacyFlag'] = $this->legacyFlag;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use stdClass;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.

final class ApiTestSerializationGetQueryParams
{

     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param ?array<int> $exploded
     * @param ?array<string> $joined
     * @param ?array<int> $spaced
     * @param ?array<string> $piped
     * @param ?array<string, string> $nested
     * @param ?array<string> $xCodes
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly ?array $exploded = null,
        private readonly ?array $joined = null,
        private readonly ?array $spaced = null,
        private readonly ?array $piped = null,
        private readonly ?array $nested = null,
        private readonly ?bool $flag = null,
        private readonly ?string $strict = null,
        private readonly ?string $raw = null,
        private readonly ?array $xCodes = null,
        private readonly ?string $session = null,
        private readonly ?ApiTestSerializationGetQueryParamsFilter $filter = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'exploded' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'exploded.*' => ['integer'],
            'joined' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'joined.*' => ['string'],
            'spaced' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'spaced.*' => ['integer'],
            'piped' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'piped.*' => ['string'],
            'nested' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'nested.*' => ['string'],
            'flag' => ['sometimes', 'boolean'],
            'strict' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            'raw' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            'X-Codes' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'X-Codes.*' => ['string'],
            'session' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            'filter' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'filter.since' => ['date_format:Y-m-d'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'nested',
        'filter',
    ];

    /**
     * The one shape Laravel's rules cannot check: `type: object` given a JSON array.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ApiTestSerializationGetQueryParams::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            exploded: ($data['exploded'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['exploded'],
            joined: ($data['joined'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['joined'],
            spaced: ($data['spaced'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['spaced'],
            piped: ($data['piped'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['piped'],
            nested: ($data['nested'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['nested'],
            flag: ($data['flag'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['flag'],
            strict: ($data['strict'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['strict'],
            raw: ($data['raw'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['raw'],
            xCodes: ($data['X-Codes'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['X-Codes'],
            session: ($data['session'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['session'],
            filter: ($data['filter'] ?? null) === null ? null : ApiTestSerializationGetQueryParamsFilter::fromValidated($data['filter']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

     * @return ?array<int>
     */
    public function getExploded(): ?array

    }

    {

    }

    public function getFilter(): ?ApiTestSerializationGetQueryParamsFilter
    {
        return $this->filter;

    {
        return in_array('filter', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];

        if ($this->isExplodedProvided()) {
            $result['exploded'] = $this->exploded === null ? null : array_values($this->exploded);
        }

        if ($this->isJoinedProvided()) {
            $result['joined'] = $this->joined === null ? null : array_values($this->joined);
        }

        if ($this->isSpacedProvided()) {
            $result['spaced'] = $this->spaced === null ? null : array_values($this->spaced);
        }

        if ($this->isPipedProvided()) {
            $result['piped'] = $this->piped === null ? null : array_values($this->piped);
        }

        if ($this->isNestedProvided()) {
            $result['nested'] = $this->nested === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->nested);
        }

        if ($this->isFlagProvided()) {
            $result['flag'] = $this->flag;
        }

        if ($this->isStrictProvided()) {
            $result['strict'] = $this->strict;
        }

        if ($this->isRawProvided()) {
            $result['raw'] = $this->raw;
        }

        if ($this->isXCodesProvided()) {
            $result['X-Codes'] = $this->xCodes === null ? null : array_values($this->xCodes);
        }

        if ($this->isSessionProvided()) {
            $result['session'] = $this->session;
        }

        if ($this->isFilterProvided()) {
            $result['filter'] = $this->filter === null ? null : ($this->filter->toArray() ?: (object)[]);
        }

        return $result;
    }

===== ApplicatorRefs.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_map;
use function array_shift;
use function array_values;
use function count;
use function explode;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function mb_strlen;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ApplicatorRefs::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ApplicatorRefs::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ApplicatorRefs
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'mustContain' => [
            'contains' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'required' => [
                    'label',
                ],
                'properties' => [
                    'label' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 2,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'positions' => [
            'prefixItems' => [
                [
                    'type' => 'object',
                    'required' => [
                        'label',
                    ],
                    'properties' => [
                        'label' => [
                            'type' => 'string',
                            'minLength' => 2,
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'rest' => [
            'prefixItems' => [
                [
                    'type' => 'string',
                ],
            ],
            'unevaluatedItems' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'required' => [
                    'label',
                ],
                'properties' => [
                    'label' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 2,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'closedPair' => [
            'items' => false,
            'prefixItems' => [
                [
                    'type' => 'string',
                ],
                [
                    'type' => 'integer',
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'conditional' => [
            'if' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'required' => [
                    'label',
                ],
                'properties' => [
                    'label' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 2,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'then' => [
                'required' => [
                    'label',
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'keyed' => [
            'dependentSchemas' => [
                'trigger' => [
                    'type' => 'object',
                    'required' => [
                        'label',
                    ],
                    'properties' => [
                        'label' => [
                            'type' => 'string',
                            'minLength' => 2,
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param array<array<string, mixed>> $mustContain
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $mustContain,
        private readonly ?array $positions = null,
        private readonly ?array $rest = null,
        private readonly ?array $closedPair = null,
        private readonly ?ApplicatorRefsConditional $conditional = null,
        private readonly ?ApplicatorRefsKeyed $keyed = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'mustContain' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'mustContain.*' => ['array'],
            'positions' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'rest' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'closedPair' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'conditional' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'conditional.label' => ['string'],
            'keyed' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'keyed.trigger' => ['string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'mustContain.*',
        'conditional',
        'keyed',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ApplicatorRefs::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            mustContain: $data['mustContain'],
            positions: ($data['positions'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['positions'],
            rest: ($data['rest'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['rest'],
            closedPair: ($data['closedPair'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['closedPair'],
            conditional: ($data['conditional'] ?? null) === null ? null : ApplicatorRefsConditional::fromValidated($data['conditional']),
            keyed: ($data['keyed'] ?? null) === null ? null : ApplicatorRefsKeyed::fromValidated($data['keyed']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getMustContain(): array
    {
        return $this->mustContain;
    }

    public function getPositions(): ?array
    {
        return $this->positions;
    }

    public function isPositionsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('positions', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getRest(): ?array
    {
        return $this->rest;
    }

    public function isRestProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('rest', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getClosedPair(): ?array
    {
        return $this->closedPair;
    }

    public function isClosedPairProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('closedPair', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getConditional(): ?ApplicatorRefsConditional
    {
        return $this->conditional;
    }

    public function isConditionalProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('conditional', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getKeyed(): ?ApplicatorRefsKeyed
    {
        return $this->keyed;
    }

    public function isKeyedProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('keyed', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['dependentSchemas'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentSchemas'] as $propertyName => $depSchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($depSchema) || !array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue, $depSchema, $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['if'] ?? null)) {
            if (self::matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['if'], $depth + 1)) {
                if (is_array($schema['then'] ?? null)) {
                    $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['then'], $path, $depth + 1)];
                }
            } elseif (is_array($schema['else'] ?? null)) {
                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['else'], $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);
        $evaluatedItemIndices = [];
        $prefixCount = 0;

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['prefixItems'] ?? null)) {
            $prefixCount = count($schema['prefixItems']);
            foreach ($schema['prefixItems'] as $index => $itemSchema) {
                if (!is_array($itemSchema) || !array_key_exists($index, $value)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value[$index], $itemSchema, $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                if ($index < $prefixCount) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($index < $prefixCount) {
                    continue;
                }
                $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['contains'] ?? null)) {
            $matchCount = 0;
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if (self::matchesOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['contains'], $depth + 1)) {
                    $matchCount++;
                    $evaluatedItemIndices[$index] = true;
                }
            }

            $minContains = 1;
            if ($matchCount < $minContains) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at least %d item(s) matching the \'contains\' schema', $path, $minContains);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && array_key_exists('unevaluatedItems', $schema)) {
            $unevaluatedItemsSchema = $schema['unevaluatedItems'];
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if (array_key_exists($index, $evaluatedItemIndices)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if ($unevaluatedItemsSchema === false) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an unevaluated item at index %s which is not allowed', $path, $index);

                    continue;
                }

                if (!is_array($unevaluatedItemsSchema)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $unevaluatedItemsSchema, $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private static function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['mustContain'] = array_map(static fn(?array $item): ?object => $item === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($item), array_values($this->mustContain));

        if ($this->isPositionsProvided()) {
            $result['positions'] = $this->positions === null ? null : array_values($this->positions);
        }

        if ($this->isRestProvided()) {
            $result['rest'] = $this->rest === null ? null : array_values($this->rest);
        }

        if ($this->isClosedPairProvided()) {
            $result['closedPair'] = $this->closedPair === null ? null : array_values($this->closedPair);
        }

        if ($this->isConditionalProvided()) {
            $result['conditional'] = $this->conditional === null ? null : ($this->conditional->toArray() ?: (object)[]);
        }

        if ($this->isKeyedProvided()) {
            $result['keyed'] = $this->keyed === null ? null : ($this->keyed->toArray() ?: (object)[]);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

            // `minProperties`, `dependentRequired`, `not`, a top-level conditional. It is asked of the
            // whole payload, and the message goes in without a field name because that is what it is
            // about. `properties` and `required` are not in this schema — the rule map owns them — so
            // nothing here is reported twice.
            foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload, self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS['x-openapi-object'], '', 0) as $error) {
                $validator->errors()->add('', str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            trigger: ($data['trigger'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['trigger'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getTrigger(): ?string
    {
        return $this->trigger;
    }

    public function isTriggerProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('trigger', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['dependentSchemas'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentSchemas'] as $propertyName => $depSchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($depSchema) || !array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue, $depSchema, $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];

        if ($this->isTriggerProvided()) {
            $result['trigger'] = $this->trigger;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.

 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own

 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class AwkwardNames
{
    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $plain,
        private readonly string $itS,
        private readonly ?string $backSlash = null,
        private readonly ?int $bothS = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'plain' => ['present', 'string'],
            'it\'s' => ['present', 'string', 'min:2'],
            'back\slash' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            'both\\\'s' => ['sometimes', 'integer', 'min:1'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            plain: $data['plain'],
            itS: $data['it\'s'],
            backSlash: ($data['back\slash'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['back\slash'],
            bothS: ($data['both\\\'s'] ?? null) === null ? null : self::toIntIfIntegral($data['both\\\'s']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getPlain(): string
    {
        return $this->plain;
    }

    public function getItS(): string
    {
        return $this->itS;
    }

    public function getBackSlash(): ?string
    {
        return $this->backSlash;
    }

    public function isBackSlashProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('back\slash', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getBothS(): ?int
    {
        return $this->bothS;
    }

    public function isBothSProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('both\\\'s', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['plain'] = $this->plain;
        $result['it\'s'] = $this->itS;

        if ($this->isBackSlashProvided()) {
            $result['back\slash'] = $this->backSlash;
        }

        if ($this->isBothSProvided()) {
            $result['both\\\'s'] = $this->bothS;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== ChainLink.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_map;
use function array_shift;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function get_object_vars;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ChainLink::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ChainLink::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ChainLink
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'tags' => [
            'items' => [
                'required' => [
                    'label',
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param ?array<ChainTag> $tags
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $code,
        private readonly ?array $tags = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'code' => ['present', 'string', 'min:3'],
            'tags' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'tags.*.label' => ['string', 'min:2'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'tags.*',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ChainLink::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            code: $data['code'],
            tags: ($data['tags'] ?? null) === null ? null : array_map(static fn(array $item): ChainTag => ChainTag::fromValidated($item), $data['tags']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getCode(): string
    {
        return $this->code;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<ChainTag>
     */
    public function getTags(): ?array
    {
        return $this->tags;
    }

    public function isTagsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('tags', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['code'] = $this->code;

        if ($this->isTagsProvided()) {
            $result['tags'] = $this->tags === null ? null : array_map(static fn(ChainTag $item): array|object => $item->toArray() ?: (object)[], array_values($this->tags));
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== ChainMiddle.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_map;
use function array_shift;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ChainMiddle::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ChainMiddle::fromValidated($request->validated());
 */
final class ChainMiddle
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'links' => [
            'items' => [
                'properties' => [
                    'tags' => [
                        'items' => [
                            'required' => [
                                'label',
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
                'required' => [
                    'code',
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * @param array<ChainLink> $links
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $links,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'links' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'links.*.code' => ['string', 'min:3'],
            'links.*.tags' => ['array', 'list'],
            'links.*.tags.*.label' => ['string', 'min:2'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'links.*',
        'links.*.tags.*',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ChainMiddle::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            links: array_map(static fn(array $item): ChainLink => ChainLink::fromValidated($item), $data['links']),
        );

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<ChainLink>
     */
    public function getLinks(): array
    {
        return $this->links;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['links'] = array_map(static fn(ChainLink $item): array|object => $item->toArray() ?: (object)[], array_values($this->links));

        return $result;
    }
}

===== Circle.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use function floor;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return Circle::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = Circle::fromValidated($request->validated());
 */
final class Circle implements Shape
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $kind,
        private readonly int $radius,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'kind' => ['present', 'string'],
            'radius' => ['present', 'integer'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            kind: $data['kind'],
            radius: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['radius']),
        );

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getKind(): string
    {
        return $this->kind;
    }

    public function getRadius(): int
    {
        return $this->radius;
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['kind'] = $this->kind;
        $result['radius'] = $this->radius;

        return $result;
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_is_list;
use function array_keys;
use function array_map;

/**

 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own

final class ContainerAliases
{
    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param array<array<string>> $listOfLists
     * @param ?array<array<string, mixed>> $listOfMaps
     * @param ?array<string, array<string>> $mapOfLists
     * @param ?array<string> $plain

        private readonly ?array $listOfMaps = null,
        private readonly ?array $mapOfLists = null,
        private readonly ?array $plain = null,

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>

    public static function rules(): array

            'listOfMaps' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'mapOfLists' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'plain' => ['sometimes'],
