    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'mapOfLists',
    ];

    /**
     * The one shape Laravel's rules cannot check: `type: object` given a JSON array.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ContainerAliases::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            listOfLists: $data['listOfLists'],
            listOfMaps: ($data['listOfMaps'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['listOfMaps'],
            mapOfLists: ($data['mapOfLists'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['mapOfLists'],
            plain: ($data['plain'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['plain'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<array<string>>
     */
    public function getListOfLists(): array
    {
        return $this->listOfLists;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getListOfMaps(): ?array
    {
        return $this->listOfMaps;
    }

    public function isListOfMapsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('listOfMaps', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string, array<string>>
     */
    public function getMapOfLists(): ?array
    {
        return $this->mapOfLists;
    }

    public function isMapOfListsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('mapOfLists', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string>
     */
    public function getPlain(): ?array
    {
        return $this->plain;
    }

    public function isPlainProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('plain', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['listOfLists'] = array_values($this->listOfLists);

        if ($this->isListOfMapsProvided()) {
            $result['listOfMaps'] = $this->listOfMaps === null ? null : array_map(static fn(?array $item): ?object => $item === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($item), array_values($this->listOfMaps));
        }

        if ($this->isMapOfListsProvided()) {
            $result['mapOfLists'] = $this->mapOfLists === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->mapOfLists);
        }

        if ($this->isPlainProvided()) {
            $result['plain'] = $this->plain === null ? null : array_values($this->plain);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [

                    'id',
                    'name',
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param array<DateTimeImmutable> $dates
     * @param array<Shape> $shapes
     * @param ?array<string, DateTimeImmutable> $datesByDay
     * @param ?array<DateTimeImmutable> $timestamps
     * @param ?array<string, Tag> $tagsByKey
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $dates,
        private readonly array $shapes,
        private readonly ?array $datesByDay = null,
        private readonly ?array $timestamps = null,
        private readonly ?array $tagsByKey = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'dates' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'dates.*' => ['string', 'date_format:Y-m-d'],
            'shapes' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'datesByDay' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'datesByDay.*' => ['string', 'date_format:Y-m-d'],
            'timestamps' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'timestamps.*' => ['string', 'date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i:sP,Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP,Y-m-d H:i:s,Y-m-d\TH:i:s'],
            'tagsByKey' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'tagsByKey.*.id' => ['integer'],
            'tagsByKey.*.name' => ['string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'shapes.*',
        'datesByDay',
        'tagsByKey',
        'tagsByKey.*',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         Containers::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            dates: array_map(static fn(string $item): DateTimeImmutable => new DateTimeImmutable($item), $data['dates']),
            shapes: array_map(static fn(array $item): Shape => match ($item['kind'] ?? null) { 'circle' => Circle::fromValidated($item), 'square' => Square::fromValidated($item), default => throw new InvalidArgumentException(sprintf('Unknown kind "%s".', (string)($item['kind'] ?? ''))) }, $data['shapes']),
            datesByDay: ($data['datesByDay'] ?? null) === null ? null : array_map(static fn(string $item): DateTimeImmutable => new DateTimeImmutable($item), $data['datesByDay']),
            timestamps: ($data['timestamps'] ?? null) === null ? null : array_map(static fn(string $item): DateTimeImmutable => new DateTimeImmutable($item), $data['timestamps']),
            tagsByKey: ($data['tagsByKey'] ?? null) === null ? null : array_map(static fn(array $item): Tag => Tag::fromValidated($item), $data['tagsByKey']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * Formatted as the schema declares it — a date stays a date, and a date-time keeps the
     * sub-second precision the payload carried.
     *
     * @return array<string>
     */
    public function getDates(): array
    {
        return array_map(
            static fn (mixed $item): mixed => $item instanceof DateTimeImmutable
                ? $item->format('Y-m-d')
                : $item,
            $this->dates,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @return array<DateTimeImmutable>
     */
    public function getDatesAsDateTime(): array
    {
        return $this->dates;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<Shape>
     */
    public function getShapes(): array
    {
        return $this->shapes;
    }

    /**
     * Formatted as the schema declares it — a date stays a date, and a date-time keeps the
     * sub-second precision the payload carried.
     *
     * @return ?array<string, string>
     */
    public function getDatesByDay(): ?array
    {
        return $this->datesByDay === null ? null : array_map(
            static fn (mixed $item): mixed => $item instanceof DateTimeImmutable
                ? $item->format('Y-m-d')
                : $item,
            $this->datesByDay,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string, DateTimeImmutable>
     */
    public function getDatesByDayAsDateTime(): ?array
    {
        return $this->datesByDay;
    }

    public function isDatesByDayProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('datesByDay', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * Formatted as the schema declares it — a date stays a date, and a date-time keeps the
     * sub-second precision the payload carried.
     *
     * @return ?array<string>
     */
    public function getTimestamps(): ?array
    {
        return $this->timestamps === null ? null : array_map(
            static fn (mixed $item): mixed => $item instanceof DateTimeImmutable
                ? ($item->format('u') === '000000'
                    ? $item->format('c')
                    : $item->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP'))
                : $item,
            $this->timestamps,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<DateTimeImmutable>
     */
    public function getTimestampsAsDateTime(): ?array
    {
        return $this->timestamps;
    }

    public function isTimestampsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('timestamps', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string, Tag>
     */
    public function getTagsByKey(): ?array
    {
        return $this->tagsByKey;
    }

    public function isTagsByKeyProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('tagsByKey', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...self::validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['dates'] = $this->getDates();
        $result['shapes'] = array_map(static fn(Shape $item): array|object => $item->toArray() ?: (object)[], array_values($this->shapes));

        if ($this->isDatesByDayProvided()) {
            $result['datesByDay'] = $this->datesByDay === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->getDatesByDay());
        }

        if ($this->isTimestampsProvided()) {
            $result['timestamps'] = $this->getTimestamps();
        }

        if ($this->isTagsByKeyProvided()) {
            $result['tagsByKey'] = $this->tagsByKey === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects(array_map(static fn(Tag $item): array|object => $item->toArray() ?: (object)[], $this->tagsByKey));
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

    }

    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.

    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {

===== Dog2.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.

===== ItemBounds.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_is_list;
use function array_keys;
use function array_shift;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function get_object_vars;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function json_decode;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ItemBounds::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ItemBounds::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ItemBounds
{
    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param array<int> $scores
     * @param ?array<string, int> $scoresByKey
     * @param ?array<string> $codes
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $scores,
        private readonly ?array $scoresByKey = null,
        private readonly ?array $codes = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'scores' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'scores.*' => ['integer', 'min:5'],
            'scoresByKey' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'scoresByKey.*' => ['integer', 'min:5'],
            'codes' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'codes.*' => ['string', 'min:2', 'regex:/^[a-z]+$/'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'scoresByKey',
    ];

    /**
     * The one shape Laravel's rules cannot check: `type: object` given a JSON array.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ItemBounds::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            scores: $data['scores'],
            scoresByKey: ($data['scoresByKey'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['scoresByKey'],
            codes: ($data['codes'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['codes'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<int>
     */
    public function getScores(): array
    {
        return $this->scores;
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string, int>
     */
    public function getScoresByKey(): ?array
    {
        return $this->scoresByKey;
    }

    public function isScoresByKeyProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('scoresByKey', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @return ?array<string>
     */
    public function getCodes(): ?array
    {
        return $this->codes;
    }

    public function isCodesProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('codes', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['scores'] = array_values($this->scores);

        if ($this->isScoresByKeyProvided()) {
            $result['scoresByKey'] = $this->scoresByKey === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->scoresByKey);
        }

        if ($this->isCodesProvided()) {
            $result['codes'] = $this->codes === null ? null : array_values($this->codes);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         NestedContainers::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());

     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire

     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.

    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {

                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['matrix'] = array_values($this->matrix);
        $result['rowsOfMaps'] = array_map(static fn(?array $item): ?object => $item === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($item), array_values($this->rowsOfMaps));

        if ($this->isListsByKeyProvided()) {
            $result['listsByKey'] = $this->listsByKey === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->listsByKey);
        }

        if ($this->isMapsByKeyProvided()) {
            $result['mapsByKey'] = $this->mapsByKey === null ? null : self::toJsonObjects($this->mapsByKey);
        }

        if ($this->isDateRowsProvided()) {
            $result['dateRows'] = $this->dateRows === null ? null : array_values($this->dateRows);
        }

        if ($this->isNumRowsProvided()) {
            $result['numRows'] = $this->numRows === null ? null : array_values($this->numRows);
        }

        if ($this->isEnumRowsProvided()) {
            $result['enumRows'] = $this->enumRows === null ? null : array_values($this->enumRows);
        }

        if ($this->isTagRowsProvided()) {
            $result['tagRows'] = $this->tagRows === null ? null : array_values($this->tagRows);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

        ];

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>

    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'mapOfNullable',
        'mapOfNullable31',
    ];

    /**
     * The one shape Laravel's rules cannot check: `type: object` given a JSON array.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         NullableContainerValues::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            listOfNullable: $data['listOfNullable'],
            mapOfNullable: ($data['mapOfNullable'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['mapOfNullable'],
            listOfNullable31: ($data['listOfNullable31'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['listOfNullable31'],
            mapOfNullable31: ($data['mapOfNullable31'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['mapOfNullable31'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    /**

    /**

    }

    public function isMapOfNullable31Provided(): bool
    {

    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {

            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by

                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;

    {

===== ObjectWideRules.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function count;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_scalar;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function mb_strlen;
use function serialize;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;
use function var_export;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ObjectWideRules::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ObjectWideRules::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ObjectWideRules
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'x-openapi-object' => [
            'minProperties' => 2,
            'dependentRequired' => [
                'code' => [
                    'extra',
                ],
            ],
            'not' => [
                'required' => [
                    'forbidden',
                ],
            ],
            'if' => [
                'required' => [
                    'kind',
                ],
            ],
            'then' => [
                'properties' => [
                    'code' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'minLength' => 3,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $kind,
        private readonly ?string $code = null,
        private readonly ?string $extra = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'kind' => ['present', 'string'],
            'code' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
            'extra' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ObjectWideRules::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                // The object's own rules share this map under a reserved key — see below.
                if ($property === 'x-openapi-object' || !array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            // What the document states about the OBJECT rather than about one property:
            // `minProperties`, `dependentRequired`, `not`, a top-level conditional. It is asked of the
            // whole payload, and the message goes in without a field name because that is what it is
            // about. `properties` and `required` are not in this schema — the rule map owns them — so
            // nothing here is reported twice.
            foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload, self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS['x-openapi-object'], '', 0) as $error) {
                $validator->errors()->add('', str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            kind: $data['kind'],
            code: ($data['code'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['code'],
            extra: ($data['extra'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['extra'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getKind(): string
    {
        return $this->kind;
    }

    public function getCode(): ?string
    {
        return $this->code;
    }

    public function isCodeProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('code', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getExtra(): ?string
    {
        return $this->extra;
    }

    public function isExtraProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('extra', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($value) || is_object($value)) {
            $structural = is_array($value) ? $value : self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);
            $valueArray = is_array($structural) ? array_filter(
                $structural,
                static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
            ) : [];
            $count = count($valueArray);
            $minItems = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minItems'] ?? null);
            if ($minItems !== null && is_array($value) && $count < $minItems) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at least %d items', $path, $minItems);
            }

            $maxItems = self::toIntConstraint($schema['maxItems'] ?? null);
            if ($maxItems !== null && is_array($value) && $count > $maxItems) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at most %d items', $path, $maxItems);
            }

            $minProperties = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minProperties'] ?? null);
            if ($minProperties !== null && $count < $minProperties) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must have at least %d %s', $path, $minProperties, $minProperties === 1 ? 'property' : 'properties');
            }

            $maxProperties = self::toIntConstraint($schema['maxProperties'] ?? null);
            if ($maxProperties !== null && $count > $maxProperties) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must have at most %d %s', $path, $maxProperties, $maxProperties === 1 ? 'property' : 'properties');
            }

            if (is_array($value) && ($schema['uniqueItems'] ?? false) === true) {
                $seen = [];
                foreach ($valueArray as $item) {
                    if (is_scalar($item) || $item === null) {
                        $fingerprint = 's:' . var_export($item, true);
                    } else {
                        // A generated DTO keeps its state private, so compare the payload it
                        // reports rather than the object, which would encode as {} every time.
                        $comparable = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($item);
                        $json = json_encode($comparable, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);
                        $fingerprint = is_string($json) ? 'j:' . $json : 'j:' . serialize($comparable);
                    }
                    if (array_key_exists($fingerprint, $seen)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain unique items', $path);
                        break;
                    }
                    $seen[$fingerprint] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['dependentRequired'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['dependentRequired'] as $propertyName => $deps) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($deps) || !array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                foreach ($deps as $depProperty) {
                    if (is_string($depProperty) && !array_key_exists($depProperty, $structuredValue)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required when %s is present', self::openApiChildPath($path, $depProperty), $propertyName);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['not'] ?? null) && self::matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['not'], $depth + 1)) {
            $errors[] = sprintf('%s must not match the \'not\' schema', $path);
        }

        if (is_array($schema['if'] ?? null)) {
            if (self::matchesOpenApiNode($value, $schema['if'], $depth + 1)) {
                if (is_array($schema['then'] ?? null)) {
                    $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['then'], $path, $depth + 1)];
                }
            } elseif (is_array($schema['else'] ?? null)) {
                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema['else'], $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private static function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'string' => is_string($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['kind'] = $this->kind;

        if ($this->isCodeProvided()) {
            $result['code'] = $this->code;
        }

        if ($this->isExtraProvided()) {
            $result['extra'] = $this->extra;
        }

        return $result;
    }

===== ReadOnlyFields.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use DateTimeImmutable;
use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

use function array_diff;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_values;
use function floor;
use function in_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return ReadOnlyFields::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = ReadOnlyFields::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class ReadOnlyFields
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'serverId' => [
            'readOnly' => true,
        ],
        'writtenAt' => [
            'readOnly' => true,
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $title,
        private readonly ?int $serverId = null,
        private readonly ?DateTimeImmutable $writtenAt = null,
        private readonly ?string $secret = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'serverId' => ['sometimes', 'integer'],
            'title' => ['present', 'string'],
            'writtenAt' => ['sometimes', 'date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i:sP,Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP,Y-m-d H:i:s,Y-m-d\TH:i:s'],
            'secret' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         ReadOnlyFields::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            serverId: null,
            title: $data['title'],
            writtenAt: null,
            secret: ($data['secret'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['secret'],
        );
        // A readOnly key is ignored on the way in, so it does not count as provided either.
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_values(array_diff(array_keys($data), ['serverId', 'writtenAt']));

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getServerId(): ?int
    {
        return $this->serverId;
    }

    public function isServerIdProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('serverId', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getTitle(): string
    {
        return $this->title;
    }

    /**
     * Formatted as the schema declares it — a date stays a date, and a date-time keeps the
     * sub-second precision the payload carried.
     */
    public function getWrittenAt(): ?string
    {
        return $this->writtenAt === null ? null : ($this->writtenAt->format('u') === '000000'
            ? $this->writtenAt->format('c')
            : $this->writtenAt->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP'));
    }

    public function getWrittenAtAsDateTime(): ?DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->writtenAt;
    }

    public function isWrittenAtProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('writtenAt', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getSecret(): ?string
    {
        return $this->secret;
    }

    public function isSecretProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('secret', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];

        if ($this->isServerIdProvided()) {
            $result['serverId'] = $this->serverId;
        }
        $result['title'] = $this->title;

        if ($this->isWrittenAtProvided()) {
            $result['writtenAt'] = $this->getWrittenAt();
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== Test2.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_map;
use function array_shift;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return Test2::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = Test2::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class Test2
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'tags' => [
            'items' => [
                'required' => [
                    'id',
                    'name',
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'user' => [
            'required' => [
                'id',
                'name',
                'pet',
                'createdAt',
            ],
            'nullable' => true,
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    /**
     * @param int $id this is id
     * @param string $name this is name
     * @param array<Tag> $tags
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly int $id,
        private readonly string $name,
        private readonly array $tags,
        private readonly Test2Types $types,
        private readonly ?User $user = null,
        private readonly ?Test2UserType $userType = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => ['present', 'integer'],
            'name' => ['present', 'string'],
            'tags' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'tags.*.id' => ['integer'],
            'tags.*.name' => ['string'],
            'types' => ['present', Rule::enum(Test2Types::class)],
            'user' => ['sometimes'],
            'user.id' => ['integer'],
            'user.name' => ['string'],
            'user.pet' => ['nullable'],
            'user.createdAt' => ['date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i:sP,Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP,Y-m-d H:i:s,Y-m-d\TH:i:s'],
            'userType' => ['sometimes', 'array'],
            'userType.region' => ['string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'tags.*',
        'user',
        'user.pet',
        'userType',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         Test2::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            id: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['id']),
            name: $data['name'],
            tags: array_map(static fn(array $item): Tag => Tag::fromValidated($item), $data['tags']),
            types: Test2Types::from($data['types']),
            user: ($data['user'] ?? null) === null ? null : User::fromValidated($data['user']),
            userType: ($data['userType'] ?? null) === null ? null : Test2UserType::fromValidated($data['userType']),
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getId(): int
    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    public function getName(): string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<Tag>
     */
    public function getTags(): array
    {
        return $this->tags;
    }

    public function getTypes(): Test2Types
    {
        return $this->types;
    }

    public function getUser(): ?User
    {
        return $this->user;
    }

    public function isUserProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('user', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getUserType(): ?Test2UserType
    {
        return $this->userType;
    }

    public function isUserTypeProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('userType', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        if ($value === null && ($schema['nullable'] ?? false) === true) {
            return [];
        }

        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['id'] = $this->id;
        $result['name'] = $this->name;
        $result['tags'] = array_map(static fn(Tag $item): array|object => $item->toArray() ?: (object)[], array_values($this->tags));
        $result['types'] = $this->types->value;

        if ($this->isUserProvided()) {
            $result['user'] = $this->user === null ? null : ($this->user->toArray() ?: (object)[]);
        }

        if ($this->isUserTypeProvided()) {
            $result['userType'] = $this->userType === null ? null : ($this->userType->toArray() ?: (object)[]);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}
