===== TestPostRequest.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_pop;
use function array_unique;
use function array_values;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function implode;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_bool;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_scalar;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function ltrim;
use function preg_match;
use function preg_replace;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_contains;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return TestPostRequest::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = TestPostRequest::fromValidated($request->validated());
 *
 * `validated()` returns only the keys the payload carried, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads —
 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).
 */
final class TestPostRequest
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'code' => [
            'oneOf' => [
                [
                    'type' => 'integer',
                    'minimum' => 10,
                    'maximum' => 100,
                ],
                [
                    'type' => 'string',
                    'format' => 'uuid',
                ],
            ],
        ],
        'endpoint' => [
            'format' => 'uri-template',
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly int $id,
        private readonly string $name,
        private readonly int|string $code,
        private readonly ?string $homepage = null,
        private readonly ?string $endpoint = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => ['present', 'integer'],
            'name' => ['present', 'string'],
            'code' => ['present'],
            'homepage' => ['sometimes', 'string', 'url'],
            'endpoint' => ['sometimes', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         TestPostRequest::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            id: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['id']),
            name: $data['name'],
            code: self::toIntIfIntegral($data['code']),
            homepage: ($data['homepage'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['homepage'],
            endpoint: ($data['endpoint'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['endpoint'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getId(): int
    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    public function getName(): string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    public function getCode(): int|string
    {
        return $this->code;
    }

    public function getHomepage(): ?string
    {
        return $this->homepage;
    }

    public function isHomepageProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('homepage', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getEndpoint(): ?string
    {
        return $this->endpoint;
    }

    public function isEndpointProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('endpoint', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($schema['oneOf'] ?? null)) {
            $matchingBranches = 0;
            $branchErrors = [];
            foreach ($schema['oneOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (is_string($branchClass = $branch['x-php-instanceof'] ?? null) && $branchClass !== '') {
                    $branchFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($branchClass, '\\');
                    if (is_object($value) && $value instanceof $branchFqcn) {
                        $matchingBranches++;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (array_key_exists('type', $branch) && !self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $branch['type'])) {
                    continue;
                }
                // Keep why each branch failed: with no match at all those reasons are far more
                // useful than "does not match any oneOf branch".
                $errorsForBranch = self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1);
                if ($errorsForBranch === []) {
                    $matchingBranches++;
                    continue;
                }

                $branchErrors = [...$branchErrors, ...$errorsForBranch];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches > 1) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s matches more than one allowed oneOf branch', $path);
            } elseif ($matchingBranches === 0) {
                $errors = $branchErrors === []
                    ? [...$errors, self::describeOpenApiUnionMismatch($path, 'oneOf', $schema['oneOf'], $value)]
                    : [...$errors, ...array_values(array_unique($branchErrors))];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches === 1) {
                $discriminatorProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorPhpProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-php-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-php-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorMap = is_array($schema['x-discriminator-map'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-map']
                    : null;

                if ($discriminatorProperty !== null && $discriminatorMap !== null && is_object($value)) {
                    $phpProperty = $discriminatorPhpProperty ?? $discriminatorProperty;
                    // The DTO keeps its state private: read the discriminator from the payload it
                    // reports, keyed by the OpenAPI name, and fall back to a public property for a
                    // hand-written object.
                    $discriminatorPayload = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);
                    $discriminatorCarrier = is_array($discriminatorPayload) ? $discriminatorPayload : [];
                    if (!array_key_exists($discriminatorProperty, $discriminatorCarrier) && !property_exists($value, $phpProperty)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator property %s is required', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                    } else {
                        $discriminatorValue = $discriminatorCarrier[$discriminatorProperty]
                            ?? (property_exists($value, $phpProperty) ? $value->{$phpProperty} : null);
                        $mapKey = is_scalar($discriminatorValue) ? (string)$discriminatorValue : null;
                        if ($mapKey === null || !array_key_exists($mapKey, $discriminatorMap)) {
                            $errors[] = sprintf(
                                '%s discriminator %s must be one of: %s',
                                $path,
                                $discriminatorProperty,
                                implode(', ', array_keys($discriminatorMap)),
                            );
                        } elseif (is_string($expectedClass = $discriminatorMap[$mapKey]) && $expectedClass !== '') {
                            $expectedFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($expectedClass, '\\');
                            if (!$value instanceof $expectedFqcn) {
                                $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator %s must match concrete class', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if ((is_int($normalizedValue) || is_float($normalizedValue)) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minimum', 'maximum'])) {
            $minimum = self::toFloatConstraint($schema['minimum'] ?? null);
            if ($minimum !== null && !($normalizedValue >= $minimum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be greater than or equal to %s', $path, self::stringifyOpenApiNumber($minimum));
            }
            $maximum = self::toFloatConstraint($schema['maximum'] ?? null);
            if ($maximum !== null && !($normalizedValue <= $maximum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be less than or equal to %s', $path, self::stringifyOpenApiNumber($maximum));
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['format'])) {
            if (is_string($format = $schema['format'] ?? null) && !self::isValidOpenApiStringFormat($normalizedValue, $format)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must match format %s', $path, $format);
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private static function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    /**
     * Every branch was gated out by its declared type, so no branch has a reason to report. Naming the
     * types the union does accept turns an unactionable sentence into one the caller can act on. Same
     * wording as the runtime validator, so a payload reads the same whichever mode answered it.
     *
     * @param array<int, mixed> $branches
     */
    private static function describeOpenApiUnionMismatch(string $path, string $kind, array $branches, mixed $value): string
    {
        $types = [];
        foreach ($branches as $branch) {
            if (!is_array($branch)) {
                continue;
            }

            $declared = is_array($branch['type'] ?? null) ? $branch['type'] : [$branch['type'] ?? null];
            foreach ($declared as $type) {
                if (is_string($type) && $type !== '' && !in_array($type, $types, true)) {
                    $types[] = $type;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($types === []) {
            return sprintf('%s does not match any %s branch', $path, $kind);
        }

        $last = array_pop($types);
        $expected = $types === [] ? $last : implode(', ', $types) . ' or ' . $last;

        return sprintf(
            '%s does not match any %s branch (expected %s, got %s)',
            $path,
            $kind,
            $expected,
            match (true) {
                $value === null => 'null',
                is_bool($value) => 'boolean',
                is_int($value) => 'integer',
                is_float($value) => 'number',
                is_string($value) => 'string',
                is_array($value) => array_is_list($value) ? 'array' : 'object',
                default => 'object',
            },
        );
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toFloatConstraint(mixed $value): ?float
    {
        return is_int($value) || is_float($value) ? (float)$value : null;
    }

    private static function stringifyOpenApiNumber(float $value): string
    {
        $rendered = json_encode($value);
        return is_string($rendered) ? $rendered : (string)$value;
    }

    private static function isValidOpenApiStringFormat(string $value, string $format): bool
    {
        return match ($format) {
            'uuid' => self::isValidOpenApiUuid($value),
            'uri-template' => self::isValidOpenApiUriTemplate($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function isValidOpenApiUuid(string $value): bool
    {
        return preg_match('/^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$/', $value) === 1;
    }

    private static function isValidOpenApiUriReference(string $value): bool
    {
        return preg_match('/[\s\x00-\x1F\x7F]/u', $value) !== 1;
    }

    private static function isValidOpenApiUriTemplate(string $value): bool
    {
        // RFC 6570: a URI-reference that may embed {expression} blocks. Every brace has to belong to a
        // well-formed expression — an optional operator followed by a comma-separated list of varspecs,
        // each a varname with an optional ":" prefix-length or "*" explode modifier. Strip the valid
        // expressions; a surviving brace means the template is malformed.
        if (!self::isValidOpenApiUriReference($value)) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!str_contains($value, '{')) {
            return !str_contains($value, '}');
        }

        $expression = '\{[+#./;?&=,!@|]?(?:[\p{L}\p{N}_%]+(?::[1-9][0-9]{0,3}|\*)?)'
            . '(?:,[\p{L}\p{N}_%]+(?::[1-9][0-9]{0,3}|\*)?)*\}';
        $stripped = preg_replace('~' . $expression . '~u', '', $value);

        return $stripped !== null && !str_contains($stripped, '{') && !str_contains($stripped, '}');
    }

    /**
     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real
     * fractional part is left alone: validation has already rejected it.
     */
    private static function toIntIfIntegral(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        return is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value
            ? (int)$value
            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['id'] = $this->id;
        $result['name'] = $this->name;
        $result['code'] = $this->code;

        if ($this->isHomepageProvided()) {
            $result['homepage'] = $this->homepage;
        }

        if ($this->isEndpointProvided()) {
            $result['endpoint'] = $this->endpoint;
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== TestPostRequestFormRequest.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

/**
 * NOTE: This FormRequest is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Type-hint it in the controller and Laravel resolves and validates it BEFORE the action body runs; a
 * failure comes out as the framework's own 422 with its error bag:
 *
 *     public function store(TestPostRequestFormRequest $request)
 *     {
 *         $dto = $request->toDto();
 *     }
 *
 * **Authorization is not generated.** An OpenAPI document describes payload shape, not policy, so this
 * class defines no `authorize()` — add one if the route needs it.
 */
final class TestPostRequestFormRequest extends FormRequest
{
    /**
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return TestPostRequest::rules();
    }

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator`. It runs against the raw
     * payload, so an undeclared key is still visible to `additionalProperties: false`.
     */
    public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
    {
        // The raw body goes along: `type: object` versus `type: array` is decided by the wire shape,
        // and the decoded payload Laravel hands the validator no longer carries it.
        TestPostRequest::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
    }

    /**
     * The validated payload as the generated DTO. `validated()` returns only the keys that were sent,
     * which is what the DTO's `isXxxProvided()` reads.
     */
    public function toDto(): TestPostRequest
    {
        /** @var array<string, mixed> $validated */
        $validated = $this->validated();

        return TestPostRequest::fromValidated($validated);
    }
}

===== TreeNode.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_pop;
use function array_shift;
use function array_unique;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function implode;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_bool;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_scalar;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function ltrim;
use function mb_strlen;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return TreeNode::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = TreeNode::fromValidated($request->validated());
 */
final class TreeNode
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'children' => [
            'additionalProperties' => [
                'oneOf' => [
                    [
                        'type' => 'object',
                        'required' => [
                            'label',
                            'children',
                        ],
                        'properties' => [
                            'label' => [
                                'type' => 'string',
                                'minLength' => 1,
                            ],
                            'children' => [
                                'type' => 'object',
                                'additionalProperties' => [
                                    'oneOf' => [
                                        [
                                            'type' => 'object',
                                            'required' => [
                                                'label',
                                                'children',
                                            ],
                                            'properties' => [
                                                'label' => [
                                                    'type' => 'string',
                                                    'minLength' => 1,
                                                ],
                                                'children' => [
                                                    'type' => 'object',
                                                ],
                                            ],
                                        ],
                                        [
                                            'type' => 'object',
                                            'additionalProperties' => [
                                                'type' => 'object',
                                                'required' => [
                                                    'id',
                                                    'name',
                                                ],
                                                'properties' => [
                                                    'id' => [
                                                        'type' => 'integer',
                                                    ],
                                                    'name' => [
                                                        'type' => 'string',
                                                    ],
                                                ],
                                            ],
                                        ],
                                    ],
                                ],
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                    [
                        'type' => 'object',
                        'additionalProperties' => [
                            'type' => 'object',
                            'required' => [
                                'id',
                                'name',
                            ],
                            'properties' => [
                                'id' => [
                                    'type' => 'integer',
                                ],
                                'name' => [
                                    'type' => 'string',
                                ],
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $children
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $label,
        private readonly array $children,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'label' => ['present', 'string', 'min:1'],
            'children' => ['present', 'array'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'children',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         TreeNode::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            label: $data['label'],
            children: $data['children'],
        );

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getLabel(): string
    {
        return $this->label;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getChildren(): array
    {
        return $this->children;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($schema['oneOf'] ?? null)) {
            $matchingBranches = 0;
            $branchErrors = [];
            foreach ($schema['oneOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (is_string($branchClass = $branch['x-php-instanceof'] ?? null) && $branchClass !== '') {
                    $branchFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($branchClass, '\\');
                    if (is_object($value) && $value instanceof $branchFqcn) {
                        $matchingBranches++;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (array_key_exists('type', $branch) && !self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $branch['type'])) {
                    continue;
                }
                // Keep why each branch failed: with no match at all those reasons are far more
                // useful than "does not match any oneOf branch".
                $errorsForBranch = self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1);
                if ($errorsForBranch === []) {
                    $matchingBranches++;
                    continue;
                }

                $branchErrors = [...$branchErrors, ...$errorsForBranch];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches > 1) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s matches more than one allowed oneOf branch', $path);
            } elseif ($matchingBranches === 0) {
                $errors = $branchErrors === []
                    ? [...$errors, self::describeOpenApiUnionMismatch($path, 'oneOf', $schema['oneOf'], $value)]
                    : [...$errors, ...array_values(array_unique($branchErrors))];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches === 1) {
                $discriminatorProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorPhpProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-php-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-php-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorMap = is_array($schema['x-discriminator-map'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-map']
                    : null;

                if ($discriminatorProperty !== null && $discriminatorMap !== null && is_object($value)) {
                    $phpProperty = $discriminatorPhpProperty ?? $discriminatorProperty;
                    // The DTO keeps its state private: read the discriminator from the payload it
                    // reports, keyed by the OpenAPI name, and fall back to a public property for a
                    // hand-written object.
                    $discriminatorPayload = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);
                    $discriminatorCarrier = is_array($discriminatorPayload) ? $discriminatorPayload : [];
                    if (!array_key_exists($discriminatorProperty, $discriminatorCarrier) && !property_exists($value, $phpProperty)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator property %s is required', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                    } else {
                        $discriminatorValue = $discriminatorCarrier[$discriminatorProperty]
                            ?? (property_exists($value, $phpProperty) ? $value->{$phpProperty} : null);
                        $mapKey = is_scalar($discriminatorValue) ? (string)$discriminatorValue : null;
                        if ($mapKey === null || !array_key_exists($mapKey, $discriminatorMap)) {
                            $errors[] = sprintf(
                                '%s discriminator %s must be one of: %s',
                                $path,
                                $discriminatorProperty,
                                implode(', ', array_keys($discriminatorMap)),
                            );
                        } elseif (is_string($expectedClass = $discriminatorMap[$mapKey]) && $expectedClass !== '') {
                            $expectedFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($expectedClass, '\\');
                            if (!$value instanceof $expectedFqcn) {
                                $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator %s must match concrete class', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...self::validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private static function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    /**
     * Every branch was gated out by its declared type, so no branch has a reason to report. Naming the
     * types the union does accept turns an unactionable sentence into one the caller can act on. Same
     * wording as the runtime validator, so a payload reads the same whichever mode answered it.
     *
     * @param array<int, mixed> $branches
     */
    private static function describeOpenApiUnionMismatch(string $path, string $kind, array $branches, mixed $value): string
    {
        $types = [];
        foreach ($branches as $branch) {
            if (!is_array($branch)) {
                continue;
            }

            $declared = is_array($branch['type'] ?? null) ? $branch['type'] : [$branch['type'] ?? null];
            foreach ($declared as $type) {
                if (is_string($type) && $type !== '' && !in_array($type, $types, true)) {
                    $types[] = $type;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($types === []) {
            return sprintf('%s does not match any %s branch', $path, $kind);
        }

        $last = array_pop($types);
        $expected = $types === [] ? $last : implode(', ', $types) . ' or ' . $last;

        return sprintf(
            '%s does not match any %s branch (expected %s, got %s)',
            $path,
            $kind,
            $expected,
            match (true) {
                $value === null => 'null',
                is_bool($value) => 'boolean',
                is_int($value) => 'integer',
                is_float($value) => 'number',
                is_string($value) => 'string',
                is_array($value) => array_is_list($value) ? 'array' : 'object',
                default => 'object',
            },
        );
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * A map is a JSON object, at every level: `type: object` says so, and an empty one must encode as
     * `{}` rather than `[]`. A list stays a list.
     */
    private static function toJsonObjects(array $value, bool $asObject = true): array|object
    {
        $converted = [];
        foreach ($value as $key => $item) {
            // A nested value decides for itself: a list stays a list, a keyed array becomes an object.
            // The caller decides for the value it owns, because an EMPTY map is a list by
            // `array_is_list()` and must still encode as `{}`.
            $converted[$key] = is_array($item)
                ? self::toJsonObjects($item, !array_is_list($item))
                : $item;
        }

        return $asObject ? (object)$converted : $converted;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['label'] = $this->label;
        $result['children'] = self::toJsonObjects($this->children);

        return $result;
    }
}

    case DOG_TYPE = 'dogType';

===== UnionUnderContainer.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;
use stdClass;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_keys;
use function array_pop;
use function array_shift;
use function array_unique;
use function array_values;
use function explode;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function implode;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_bool;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_scalar;
use function is_string;
use function json_decode;
use function ltrim;
use function mb_strlen;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

/**

 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own

 * so an absent optional field and one sent as `null` stay distinguishable (PATCH semantics).

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * The payload keys that were actually sent, which is what `isXxxProvided()` reads. Not a constructor
     * parameter: the constructor takes the schema's properties and nothing else. `fromValidated()` is the
     * only hydrator and sets this itself; a hand-built instance simply reports nothing as provided.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private array $providedOpenApiKeys = [];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly TreeNode $tree,
        private readonly ?array $chain = null,
        private readonly ?array $chainAllOf = null,
        private readonly ?array $listOfUnions = null,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'tree' => ['present'],
            'tree.label' => ['string', 'min:1'],
            'tree.children' => ['array'],
            'chain' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'chainAllOf' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
            'listOfUnions' => ['sometimes', 'array', 'list'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Paths whose schema is `type: object`. A `*` segment matches every index of an array.
     *
     * @var array<int, string>
     */
    private const array OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS = [
        'tree',
        'tree.children',
    ];

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         UnionUnderContainer::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     * `$rawJson` is the UNDECODED body. `type: object` versus `type: array` is a property of the wire
     * shape, and PHP loses it — a JSON object keyed 0..n-1 and a JSON array become the same list — so
     * that one check reads the raw body. Omit the argument and it is skipped; everything else still runs.
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }

            if ($rawJson !== null && $rawJson !== '') {
                self::assertRawObjectShapes($validator, $rawJson);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Adds an error for every `type: object` path the raw body sent as a JSON array.
     */
    private static function assertRawObjectShapes(Validator $validator, string $rawJson): void
    {
        // Decoded WITHOUT the assoc flag on purpose: that is what keeps a JSON object apart from a
        // JSON array. Laravel's own `$validator->getData()` cannot answer this question.
        $decoded = json_decode($rawJson, false);
        if (!$decoded instanceof stdClass) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::OPENAPI_OBJECT_PATHS as $path) {
            foreach (self::rawNodesAt($decoded, explode('.', $path), '') as $foundPath => $node) {
                if (is_array($node)) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($foundPath, sprintf('%s expects object, got array.', $foundPath));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every node a (possibly wildcarded) path reaches, keyed by the concrete path that got there.
     *
     * @param array<int, string> $segments
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private static function rawNodesAt(mixed $node, array $segments, string $path): array
    {
        if ($segments === []) {
            return [$path => $node];
        }

        $segment = array_shift($segments);
        if ($segment === '*') {
            // `*` stands for both an array index and a map key, so a JSON object node is walked too.
            if ($node instanceof stdClass) {
                $node = get_object_vars($node);
            }
            if (!is_array($node)) {
                return [];
            }

            $found = [];
            foreach ($node as $index => $item) {
                $itemPath = $path === '' ? (string)$index : $path . '.' . $index;
                foreach (self::rawNodesAt($item, $segments, $itemPath) as $deepPath => $deepNode) {
                    $found[$deepPath] = $deepNode;
                }
            }

            return $found;
        }

        if (!$node instanceof stdClass || !property_exists($node, $segment)) {
            return [];
        }

        return self::rawNodesAt($node->{$segment}, $segments, $path === '' ? $segment : $path . '.' . $segment);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            tree: TreeNode::fromValidated($data['tree']),
            chain: ($data['chain'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['chain'],
            chainAllOf: ($data['chainAllOf'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['chainAllOf'],
            listOfUnions: ($data['listOfUnions'] ?? null) === null ? null : $data['listOfUnions'],
        );
        $dto->providedOpenApiKeys = array_keys($data);

        return $dto;
    }

    public function getTree(): TreeNode
    {
        return $this->tree;
    }

    public function getChain(): ?array
    {
        return $this->chain;
    }

    public function isChainProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('chain', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getChainAllOf(): ?array
    {
        return $this->chainAllOf;
    }

    public function isChainAllOfProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('chainAllOf', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    public function getListOfUnions(): ?array
    {
        return $this->listOfUnions;
    }

    public function isListOfUnionsProvided(): bool
    {
        return in_array('listOfUnions', $this->providedOpenApiKeys, true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($schema['oneOf'] ?? null)) {
            $matchingBranches = 0;
            $branchErrors = [];
            foreach ($schema['oneOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (is_string($branchClass = $branch['x-php-instanceof'] ?? null) && $branchClass !== '') {
                    $branchFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($branchClass, '\\');
                    if (is_object($value) && $value instanceof $branchFqcn) {
                        $matchingBranches++;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (array_key_exists('type', $branch) && !self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $branch['type'])) {
                    continue;
                }
                // Keep why each branch failed: with no match at all those reasons are far more
                // useful than "does not match any oneOf branch".
                $errorsForBranch = self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1);
                if ($errorsForBranch === []) {
                    $matchingBranches++;
                    continue;
                }

                $branchErrors = [...$branchErrors, ...$errorsForBranch];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches > 1) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s matches more than one allowed oneOf branch', $path);
            } elseif ($matchingBranches === 0) {
                $errors = $branchErrors === []
                    ? [...$errors, self::describeOpenApiUnionMismatch($path, 'oneOf', $schema['oneOf'], $value)]
                    : [...$errors, ...array_values(array_unique($branchErrors))];
            }

            if ($matchingBranches === 1) {
                $discriminatorProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorPhpProperty = is_string($schema['x-discriminator-php-property'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-php-property']
                    : null;
                $discriminatorMap = is_array($schema['x-discriminator-map'] ?? null)
                    ? $schema['x-discriminator-map']
                    : null;

                if ($discriminatorProperty !== null && $discriminatorMap !== null && is_object($value)) {
                    $phpProperty = $discriminatorPhpProperty ?? $discriminatorProperty;
                    // The DTO keeps its state private: read the discriminator from the payload it
                    // reports, keyed by the OpenAPI name, and fall back to a public property for a
                    // hand-written object.
                    $discriminatorPayload = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);
                    $discriminatorCarrier = is_array($discriminatorPayload) ? $discriminatorPayload : [];
                    if (!array_key_exists($discriminatorProperty, $discriminatorCarrier) && !property_exists($value, $phpProperty)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator property %s is required', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                    } else {
                        $discriminatorValue = $discriminatorCarrier[$discriminatorProperty]
                            ?? (property_exists($value, $phpProperty) ? $value->{$phpProperty} : null);
                        $mapKey = is_scalar($discriminatorValue) ? (string)$discriminatorValue : null;
                        if ($mapKey === null || !array_key_exists($mapKey, $discriminatorMap)) {
                            $errors[] = sprintf(
                                '%s discriminator %s must be one of: %s',
                                $path,
                                $discriminatorProperty,
                                implode(', ', array_keys($discriminatorMap)),
                            );
                        } elseif (is_string($expectedClass = $discriminatorMap[$mapKey]) && $expectedClass !== '') {
                            $expectedFqcn = __NAMESPACE__ . '\\' . ltrim($expectedClass, '\\');
                            if (!$value instanceof $expectedFqcn) {
                                $errors[] = sprintf('%s discriminator %s must match concrete class', $path, $discriminatorProperty);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = $typeConstraint === 'array' && is_array($normalizedValue)
                        ? sprintf('%s must be a JSON array (list with sequential keys), got an associative array', $path)
                        : sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && self::schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && ($schema['items'] ?? null) === false) {
            foreach (array_keys($value) as $index) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s has an item at index %s which the schema does not allow', $path, $index);
            }
        }

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...self::validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['allOf'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['allOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1)];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['anyOf'] ?? null)) {
            $anyOfMatched = false;
            $anyOfErrors = [];
            foreach ($schema['anyOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (array_key_exists('type', $branch) && !self::matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $branch['type'])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errorsForBranch = self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1);
                if ($errorsForBranch === []) {
                    $anyOfMatched = true;
                    break;
                }

                $anyOfErrors = [...$anyOfErrors, ...$errorsForBranch];
            }

            if (!$anyOfMatched) {
                $errors = $anyOfErrors === []
                    ? [...$errors, self::describeOpenApiUnionMismatch($path, 'anyOf', $schema['anyOf'], $value)]
                    : [...$errors, ...array_values(array_unique($anyOfErrors))];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...self::validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, self::openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     */
    private static function matchesOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, int $depth): bool
    {
        return self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $schema, 'payload', $depth) === [];
    }

    /**
     * Every branch was gated out by its declared type, so no branch has a reason to report. Naming the
     * types the union does accept turns an unactionable sentence into one the caller can act on. Same
     * wording as the runtime validator, so a payload reads the same whichever mode answered it.
     *
     * @param array<int, mixed> $branches
     */
    private static function describeOpenApiUnionMismatch(string $path, string $kind, array $branches, mixed $value): string
    {
        $types = [];
        foreach ($branches as $branch) {
            if (!is_array($branch)) {
                continue;
            }

            $declared = is_array($branch['type'] ?? null) ? $branch['type'] : [$branch['type'] ?? null];
            foreach ($declared as $type) {
                if (is_string($type) && $type !== '' && !in_array($type, $types, true)) {
                    $types[] = $type;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($types === []) {
            return sprintf('%s does not match any %s branch', $path, $kind);
        }

        $last = array_pop($types);
        $expected = $types === [] ? $last : implode(', ', $types) . ' or ' . $last;

        return sprintf(
            '%s does not match any %s branch (expected %s, got %s)',
            $path,
            $kind,
            $expected,
            match (true) {
                $value === null => 'null',
                is_bool($value) => 'boolean',
                is_int($value) => 'integer',
                is_float($value) => 'number',
                is_string($value) => 'string',
                is_array($value) => array_is_list($value) ? 'array' : 'object',
                default => 'object',
            },
        );
    }

    private static function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'array' => is_array($value) && array_is_list($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['tree'] = $this->tree->toArray() ?: (object)[];

        if ($this->isChainProvided()) {
            $result['chain'] = $this->chain === null ? null : array_values($this->chain);
        }

        if ($this->isChainAllOfProvided()) {
            $result['chainAllOf'] = $this->chainAllOf === null ? null : array_values($this->chainAllOf);
        }

        if ($this->isListOfUnionsProvided()) {
            $result['listOfUnions'] = $this->listOfUnions === null ? null : array_values($this->listOfUnions);
        }

        return $result;
    }
}

===== UniqueScalars.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_values;
use function count;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_scalar;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function serialize;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;
use function var_export;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Laravel mode: the class needs nothing installed — `rules()` is fed to the framework's own
 * validator, and `fromValidated()` builds this object from `$request->validated()`:
 *
 *     final class StoreRequest extends FormRequest
 *     {
 *         public function rules(): array
 *         {
 *             return UniqueScalars::rules();
 *         }
 *     }
 *
 *     $dto = UniqueScalars::fromValidated($request->validated());
 */
final class UniqueScalars
{
    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'tags' => [
            'uniqueItems' => true,
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /**
     * @param array<string> $tags
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $tags,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Validation rules for the framework's validator, keyed by the OpenAPI property names — which is
     * what the request payload carries, not the PHP property names.
     *
     * @return array<string, array<int, mixed>>
     */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'tags' => ['present', 'array', 'list'],
            'tags.*' => ['string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The half of the schema Laravel's rule vocabulary cannot express — composition, conditionals,
     * `contains`, `unevaluated*`, `content*`, `propertyNames`, `discriminator` — checked against the
     * raw payload the validator already holds.
     *
     * Hook it up once, in the FormRequest:
     *
     *     public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
     *     {
     *         UniqueScalars::withValidator($validator, $this->getContent());
     *     }
     *
     */
    public static function withValidator(Validator $validator, ?string $rawJson = null): void
    {
        $validator->after(static function (Validator $validator) use ($rawJson): void {
            /** @var array<string, mixed> $payload */
            $payload = $validator->getData();

            foreach (self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS as $property => $schema) {
                if (!array_key_exists($property, $payload)) {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (self::validateOpenApiNode($payload[$property], $schema, $property, 0) as $error) {
                    $validator->errors()->add($property, str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data the output of `$request->validated()`
     */
    public static function fromValidated(array $data): self
    {
        $dto = new self(
            tags: $data['tags'],
        );

        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string>
     */
    public function getTags(): array
    {
        return $this->tags;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($value) || is_object($value)) {
            $structural = is_array($value) ? $value : self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);
            $valueArray = is_array($structural) ? array_filter(
                $structural,
                static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
            ) : [];
            $count = count($valueArray);
            $minItems = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minItems'] ?? null);
            if ($minItems !== null && is_array($value) && $count < $minItems) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at least %d items', $path, $minItems);
            }

            $maxItems = self::toIntConstraint($schema['maxItems'] ?? null);
            if ($maxItems !== null && is_array($value) && $count > $maxItems) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain at most %d items', $path, $maxItems);
            }

            $minProperties = self::toIntConstraint($schema['minProperties'] ?? null);
            if ($minProperties !== null && $count < $minProperties) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must have at least %d %s', $path, $minProperties, $minProperties === 1 ? 'property' : 'properties');
            }

            $maxProperties = self::toIntConstraint($schema['maxProperties'] ?? null);
            if ($maxProperties !== null && $count > $maxProperties) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must have at most %d %s', $path, $maxProperties, $maxProperties === 1 ? 'property' : 'properties');
            }

            if (is_array($value) && ($schema['uniqueItems'] ?? false) === true) {
                $seen = [];
                foreach ($valueArray as $item) {
                    if (is_scalar($item) || $item === null) {
                        $fingerprint = 's:' . var_export($item, true);
                    } else {
                        // A generated DTO keeps its state private, so compare the payload it
                        // reports rather than the object, which would encode as {} every time.
                        $comparable = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($item);
                        $json = json_encode($comparable, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);
                        $fingerprint = is_string($json) ? 'j:' . $json : 'j:' . serialize($comparable);
                    }
                    if (array_key_exists($fingerprint, $seen)) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s must contain unique items', $path);
                        break;
                    }
                    $seen[$fingerprint] = true;
                }
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private static function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private static function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['tags'] = array_values($this->tags);

        return $result;
    }
}

    {

     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private static function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        if ($value === null && ($schema['nullable'] ?? false) === true) {
            return [];
        }

        $structuredValue = self::normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {

                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', self::openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }

        if (is_array($schema['allOf'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['allOf'] as $branch) {
                if (!is_array($branch)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $errors = [...$errors, ...self::validateOpenApiNode($value, $branch, $path, $depth + 1)];

    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;

        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private static function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

     * JSON Schema 2020-12 §6.1.1 counts a number with a zero fractional part as an integer, so `42.0` is
     * a valid `integer` payload — and PHP decodes it to a float an `int` property cannot hold. A real

    {

            : $value;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['id'] = $this->id;
        $result['name'] = $this->name;
        $result['pet'] = $this->pet === null ? null : ($this->pet->toArray() ?: (object)[]);

    public function getMeow(): string
    {
        return $this->meow;
    }

    public function getBark(): string
    {
        return $this->bark;
    }

    public function getTest(): string
    {
        return $this->test;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        $result = [];
        $result['name'] = $this->name;
        $result['meow'] = $this->meow;
        $result['bark'] = $this->bark;
        $result['test'] = $this->test;

        return $result;
    }
}
